The provided excerpt shows the **EXTI_PR** (pending register) bitfields **PR17..PR0** each annotated as **`rc_w1`**. For example, **PR9** appears in the list `PR15 ... PR10 PR9 PR8 ...` and is marked `rc_w1`.

In ST register notation, **`rc_w1`** means **read/clear by writing 1**: you can read the bit, and you clear it by writing a 1. This is **not** a general “read-write” field where writing 0/1 sets the stored value; writing 1 performs a clear action.

Therefore, the fact that **EXTI.PR.PR9 access is “read-write”** is **false**; it should be characterized as **readable, cleared by write-1 (RC_W1)**.
